    @observed("formal_project", lambda a: pathlib.Path(a["project_root"]) / ".cgkn" / "agent-kit", expose=True)
    def run_formal_generation_once(
        project_root: str | pathlib.Path,
        *,
        goal: str,
        provider_mode: str = "live",
        model: str = "gpt-4.1",
        repair_model: str = "",
        timeout: int = 1800,
        replay_dir: str | pathlib.Path | None = None,
        result_path: str | pathlib.Path | None = None,
        codex_capability_brief: str | pathlib.Path | None = None,
        observed_failure: str | pathlib.Path | None = None,
        on_started: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Run/resume one production conversation generation turn synchronously.
    
        This is a local interface adapter to the sole reviewed Codex conversation
        route.  It does not add a generator, verifier, packager, or delivery exit.
        """
        root = pathlib.Path(project_root).resolve()
        artifact_dir = root / ".cgkn" / "agent-kit"
        core_profile = root / '.cgkn' / 'project-session.json'
        if core_profile.exists():
            # A configured production session uses the existing core directly.
            # Never fall back to a legacy whole-project repair loop after failure.
            if provider_mode != 'live' or replay_dir is not None:
                raise ValueError('project-session profile requires its recorded provider route')
            if observed_failure is not None or codex_capability_brief is not None:
                raise ValueError('legacy repair/brief input cannot override frozen project-session context')
            service = SoftwareProjectGenerationService(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3],
                                                        artifact_dir / 'project_sessions')
            result = service.run_profile(core_profile, goal=goal, on_started=on_started)
            output = pathlib.Path(result_path).resolve() if result_path else artifact_dir / 'generation_result.json'
            output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            output.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
            return result
>       raise ValueError('PROJECT_SESSION_PROFILE_REQUIRED: configure .cgkn/project-session.json; legacy project lifecycle is retired')
E       ValueError: PROJECT_SESSION_PROFILE_REQUIRED: configure .cgkn/project-session.json; legacy project lifecycle is retired

self = WindowsPath('artifacts/streaming-live-acceptance-20261029/paid-plan.json')
mode = 'r', buffering = -1, encoding = 'locale', errors = None, newline = None

    def open(self, mode='r', buffering=-1, encoding=None,
             errors=None, newline=None):
        """
        Open the file pointed by this path and return a file object, as
        the built-in open() function does.
        """

tests\providers\test_streaming_progress_only.py:112: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

events = [SSEEvent(data='{"id": "req", "model": "fixture", "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {"content": "partial"}, "finish_reason": null}]}', event='message', event_id=''), SSEEvent(data='[DONE]', event='message', event_id='')]

    def collect_chat_completions(
        events: Iterable[SSEEvent], *, limits: StreamLimits | None = None,
        on_progress: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
    ) -> StreamedCompletion:
        """Only [DONE] + stop + nonempty content constitutes a completed Chat stream."""
        limits = limits or StreamLimits()
        parts: list[str] = []
        size = 0
        model = ""
        request_id = ""
        usage: dict[str, Any] = {}
        finish: str | None = None
        reason_chars = 0
        event_count = 0
        done = False
        meaningful_events = 0
        for evt in events:
            event_count += 1
            if event_count > limits.max_events:
                raise StreamError("stream_too_many_events")
            if done:
                raise StreamError("stream_data_after_done")
            if evt.data.strip() == "[DONE]":
                done = True
                break
            doc = _object(evt.data)
            if "error" in doc and doc["error"] is not None:
                raise StreamError("stream_upstream_error")
            if doc.get("id"):
                current_id = str(doc["id"])
                if request_id and request_id != current_id:
                    raise StreamError("stream_mixed_request_ids")
                request_id = current_id
            if doc.get("model"):
                current_model = str(doc["model"])

    def collect_chat_completions(
        events: Iterable[SSEEvent], *, limits: StreamLimits | None = None,
        on_progress: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
    ) -> StreamedCompletion:
        """Only [DONE] + stop + nonempty content constitutes a completed Chat stream."""
        limits = limits or StreamLimits()
        parts: list[str] = []
        size = 0
        model = ""
        request_id = ""
        usage: dict[str, Any] = {}
        finish: str | None = None
        reason_chars = 0
        event_count = 0
        done = False
        meaningful_events = 0
        for evt in events:
            event_count += 1
            if event_count > limits.max_events:
                raise StreamError("stream_too_many_events")
            if done:
                raise StreamError("stream_data_after_done")
            if evt.data.strip() == "[DONE]":
                done = True
                break

                raise StreamError("stream_upstream_error")

                current_id = str(doc["id"])
                if request_id and request_id != current_id:
                    raise StreamError("stream_mixed_request_ids")
                request_id = current_id
            if doc.get("model"):
                current_model = str(doc["model"])
                if model and model != current_model:
                    raise StreamError("stream_mixed_models")
                model = current_model
            if doc.get("usage") is not None:
                usage = _usage(doc["usage"])
            meaningful = False
            choices = doc.get("choices", [])

                raise StreamError("stream_invalid_choices")

                    raise StreamError("stream_tool_calls_require_explicit_contract")
                if delta.get('refusal'):

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/sunom/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-sunom/pytest-23496/test_completed_schema_rejectio0')

    def test_completed_schema_rejection_reaches_next_project_decision(tmp_path):
        from cgkn.providers.gateway_client import GatewayCompletionError
        from cgkn.agent import AgentProjectLoop
        from cgkn.runtime import DAGRunner
        from cgkn.state import FileRunStore
        from cgkn.executors import ExecutorRegistry
        class RejectThenEscalate:
            calls=0
            def complete(self, **kwargs):
                self.calls+=1
                if self.calls==1:
                    error=GatewayCompletionError('schema_validation_failed','completed output rejected',outcome_unknown=False)
                    error.completed_response={'output_text':json.dumps({'edits':[{'action':'add_node'}]}),
                        'model':'actual-version','id':'original-request','usage':{'input_tokens':10,'output_tokens':5},
                        'cgkn_model_identity':{'actual_model':'actual-version'}}
                    raise error
                context=json.loads(kwargs['user']);feedback=context['failure_details']['decision_validation']
                assert feedback['failure_domain']=='MODEL_OUTPUT'
                assert feedback['model']=='actual-version' and feedback['request_id']=='original-request'
                assert feedback['usage']['output_tokens']==5 and not feedback['outcome_unknown']
                assert 'required property' in feedback['error'] and 'action' in feedback['raw_response']
                return completion(decision(run=False,escalate=True))
        client=RejectThenEscalate();store=FileRunStore(tmp_path)
>       loop=AgentProjectLoop(AgentComposer(client,ModelRoleConfig('agent','fixture')),
            DAGRunner(ExecutorRegistry(),store),store,known_capabilities=set(),worker_model="fixture",max_decisions=2)

    def test_failure_evaluation_returns_to_real_planner_loop(tmp_path):

                return BlockExecutionResult(project_id=request.project_id, run_id=request.run_id,
                    node_id=request.node_id, attempt_id=request.attempt_id, executor_ref=request.executor_ref,
                    status=BlockStatus.FAILED, error=ExecutionError(ExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_OUTPUT,
                        'current object mismatch'))
    
        class Client:
            def complete(self, **kwargs):
                prompt = json.loads(kwargs['user'])
                sent.append((kwargs['schema_name'], prompt))
                if kwargs['schema_name'] == 'cgkn_task_evaluation':
                    value = dict(observations='receipt reports object mismatch',
                        evidence_refs=[prompt['facts']['receipt_ref']], hypothesis='identity selection may be wrong',
                        uncertainty='unconfirmed cause', required_capabilities=['work@1'],
                        candidate_lesson='Check object identity against current input', recommendation='m2')
                else:
                    number = prompt['decision_number']
                    edits = []
                    if number == 1:
                        node, capability, config = 'work', 'work@1', {'executor_ref': 'fixture@1'}
                    elif number == 2:
                        facts = prompt['task_feedback']['items'][0]
                        assert facts['status'] == 'FAILED'
                        node, capability, config = 'evaluate', 'task.evaluate@1', {
                            'executor_ref': 'task.evaluate@1', 'meta_operation': True,
                            'inputs': {'feedback_identity': facts['identity']}}
                    else:
                        assert any(item['evaluation'] for item in prompt['task_feedback']['items'])
                    if number < 3:
                        config=probe_node(node,capability_ref=capability,config=config).as_dict()['config']
                        edits = [dict(edit_id='e'+str(number), operation='add_node', node_id=node,
                            payload=dict(capability_ref=capability, parent_id='', depends_on=[], config=config),
                            provenance={'actor': 'agent'})]
                    value = dict(schema_version='1.0', decision_id='d'+str(number), summary='controlled', reason='evidence',
                        edits=edits, request_run=number < 3, finish=number == 3,
                        needs_escalation=False, escalation_reason='')
                return ProviderCompletion(json.dumps(value), 'controlled-evaluator' if kwargs['schema_name']=='cgkn_task_evaluation' else 'controlled-planner')
    
        store = FileRunStore(tmp_path)
        client = Client()

    def test_milestone_compiler_worker_independent_failure_repair_and_checkpoint(tmp_path,entry,monkeypatch):
        workspace=tmp_path/'workspace';workspace.mkdir()
        store=FileRunStore(tmp_path/'state')

                                  {'independent':'project.acceptance@1' if entry in {'profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'} else 'measure@1'})
        acceptance.bind_subjects({'module':'NOT_YET_PRODUCED'})
        class Measure:
            descriptor=ExecutorDescriptor('measure@1','deterministic',('*',))
            def execute(self, request):
                goal=next(g for g in acceptance.view()['goals'] if g['id']==request.task_context.node_config['goal_id'])
                path=workspace/goal['milestone_contract']['design']['artifacts'][0]['path']
                digest=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                actual=runpy.run_path(str(path))['value']()
                subjects={p:digest for p in goal['subject_refs']}
                acceptance.bind_subjects({**acceptance.view()['subject_identities'],**subjects})
                return BlockExecutionResult(project_id=request.project_id,run_id=request.run_id,
                    node_id=request.node_id,attempt_id=request.attempt_id,executor_ref='measure@1',
                    status=BlockStatus.SUCCEEDED,outputs={'goal_check':{'goal_id':goal['id'],
                        'condition_hash':identity(goal['acceptance']),'subjects':subjects,
                        'execution':'VALID','evidence_refs':[str(path)],'observed':{'actual':actual,'expected':7},
                        'verdict':'VERIFIED' if actual==7 else 'FAILED'}})
        def valid_source(root,contract):
            import ast
            files=list(contract['target_scope'])
            for relative in files: ast.parse((root/relative).read_text())
            return {'passed':True,'observed':{'parsed':files},'artifacts':files}
        def snapshot(contract):
            return {p:hashlib.sha256((workspace/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() if (workspace/p).is_file() else None
                    for p in contract['target_scope']}
        def envelope(request,contract):
            measured=request.as_dict()['input_values']['execution_receipt']['outputs']['goal_check']
            return {'passed':all(acceptance.view()['subject_identities'].get(k)==v for k,v in measured['subjects'].items()),
                    'observed':measured,'evidence_refs':[dict(request.input_values['execution_receipt_ref'])]}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('entry',['session','application_service','profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'])
    def test_milestone_compiler_worker_independent_failure_repair_and_checkpoint(tmp_path,entry,monkeypatch):
        workspace=tmp_path/'workspace';workspace.mkdir()
        store=FileRunStore(tmp_path/'state')
        acceptance=GoalAcceptance(store,'p','r',{'R-value':{'must':True,'checker_keys':['independent']}},
                                  {'independent':'project.acceptance@1' if entry in {'profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'} else 'measure@1'})
        acceptance.bind_subjects({'module':'NOT_YET_PRODUCED'})
        class Measure:
            descriptor=ExecutorDescriptor('measure@1','deterministic',('*',))
            def execute(self, request):
                goal=next(g for g in acceptance.view()['goals'] if g['id']==request.task_context.node_config['goal_id'])
                path=workspace/goal['milestone_contract']['design']['artifacts'][0]['path']
                digest=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                actual=runpy.run_path(str(path))['value']()
                subjects={p:digest for p in goal['subject_refs']}
                acceptance.bind_subjects({**acceptance.view()['subject_identities'],**subjects})
                return BlockExecutionResult(project_id=request.project_id,run_id=request.run_id,
                    node_id=request.node_id,attempt_id=request.attempt_id,executor_ref='measure@1',
                    status=BlockStatus.SUCCEEDED,outputs={'goal_check':{'goal_id':goal['id'],
                        'condition_hash':identity(goal['acceptance']),'subjects':subjects,
                        'execution':'VALID','evidence_refs':[str(path)],'observed':{'actual':actual,'expected':7},
                        'verdict':'VERIFIED' if actual==7 else 'FAILED'}})
        def valid_source(root,contract):
            import ast
            files=list(contract['target_scope'])
            for relative in files: ast.parse((root/relative).read_text())
            return {'passed':True,'observed':{'parsed':files},'artifacts':files}
        def snapshot(contract):
            return {p:hashlib.sha256((workspace/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() if (workspace/p).is_file() else None
                    for p in contract['target_scope']}
        def envelope(request,contract):
            measured=request.as_dict()['input_values']['execution_receipt']['outputs']['goal_check']
            return {'passed':all(acceptance.view()['subject_identities'].get(k)==v for k,v in measured['subjects'].items()),
                    'observed':measured,'evidence_refs':[dict(request.input_values['execution_receipt_ref'])]}
        client=FakeModels();events=[]
        registry=ExecutorRegistry()
        registry.register(Measure())
        registry.register(FileDeliverableChecker(workspace,{'source':valid_source}))
        registry.register(SubjectDeliverableChecker(snapshot,{'envelope':envelope}))
        catalog={'code@1':{'executor_ref':'llm.cheap@1','semantic_task_kinds':[2,3],
                           'checker_ref':'source','acceptance':['source'],'effect_domains':['source'],
                           'description':'Bounded pure function implementation and repair'},
                 'measure@1':{'executor_ref':'measure@1','semantic_task_kinds':[4],
                              'checker_ref':'measurement','acceptance':['envelope'],'effect_domains':['measurement'],
                              'description':'Independent current-object business checker'}}
        intent={'overall_goal':'return seven','user_visible_outcomes':['value() returns 7'],'constraints':['offline'],
                'forbidden_operations':['network'],'delivery_expectations':['checked local module'],
                'major_acceptance_categories':['function result']}
        bindings={'source':'deliverable.files@1','measurement':'deliverable.subjects@1'}
        if entry=='session':
            registry.register(LLMExecutor(workspace,client,ModelRoleConfig('worker','offline-worker'),
                                          semantic_protocol=True,protocol_trace=events.append))
            session=ProjectSession(AgentComposer(client,ModelRoleConfig('agent','offline-flash'),
                planner_mode='MILESTONE_MODE',protocol_trace=events.append),
                DAGRunner(registry,store,deliverable_checkers=bindings),
                store,known_capabilities=set(catalog),worker_model='offline-worker',capability_catalog=catalog,
                acceptance=acceptance,continuous=True,max_decisions=1,
                subject_snapshot=lambda paths:snapshot({'target_scope':paths}))
            result=session.run(project_id='p',run_id='r',goal='return seven',project_intent=intent)
        else:
            from pathlib import Path
            from cgkn.application.software_project.service import SoftwareProjectGenerationService
            service=SoftwareProjectGenerationService(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2],store.root,client=client)
            if entry in {'profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'}:
                consumer=tmp_path/'consumer.py'
                consumer.write_text("import json,sys,pathlib,runpy\n"
                    "r=json.loads(pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n"
                    "p=pathlib.Path(r['workspace'])/next(iter(r['subjects']))\n"
                    "v=runpy.run_path(str(p))['value']()\n"
                    "print(json.dumps({'execution':'VALID','observed':{'value':v==7,'actual':v}}))\n")
                profile=tmp_path/'controller/.cgkn/project-session.json'
                profile.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
                profile.write_text(json.dumps({'kind':'CGKNProjectSessionProfile','schema_version':'1.0',
                    'project_id':'p','run_id':'r','workspace':str(workspace),'intent':intent,

                if entry in {'conversation','api_job','local_cli'}:

                    if entry=='conversation':
                        document=codex_conversation.run_formal_generation_once(tmp_path/'controller',goal='return seven')

                from scripts.canonical_evidence_hash import candidate_source_manifest

                assert '.cgkn-publication.lock' not in manifest
                origins=gate.delivery_sources(acceptance,workspace,manifest)
                assert origins['status']=='TRUSTED'
                assert not origins['real_provider_backed'] and not origins['delivery_authorized']
                assert set(origins['source_origins'])==set(manifest)
                from scripts import production_candidate_provenance as production
                delivery=production.produce_project_session(store,'p','r',candidate_dir=workspace,commit_sha='offline-fixture')
                assert delivery['schema_version']=='2.0' and delivery['status']=='NEEDS_REWORK'
                assert 'real_provider_planner_and_worker_required' in delivery['failures']

monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x0000018E6D20F0E0>

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('entry',['session','application_service','profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'])
    def test_milestone_compiler_worker_independent_failure_repair_and_checkpoint(tmp_path,entry,monkeypatch):
        workspace=tmp_path/'workspace';workspace.mkdir()
        store=FileRunStore(tmp_path/'state')
        acceptance=GoalAcceptance(store,'p','r',{'R-value':{'must':True,'checker_keys':['independent']}},
                                  {'independent':'project.acceptance@1' if entry in {'profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'} else 'measure@1'})
        acceptance.bind_subjects({'module':'NOT_YET_PRODUCED'})
        class Measure:
            descriptor=ExecutorDescriptor('measure@1','deterministic',('*',))
            def execute(self, request):
                goal=next(g for g in acceptance.view()['goals'] if g['id']==request.task_context.node_config['goal_id'])
                path=workspace/goal['milestone_contract']['design']['artifacts'][0]['path']
                digest=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                actual=runpy.run_path(str(path))['value']()
                subjects={p:digest for p in goal['subject_refs']}
                acceptance.bind_subjects({**acceptance.view()['subject_identities'],**subjects})
                return BlockExecutionResult(project_id=request.project_id,run_id=request.run_id,
                    node_id=request.node_id,attempt_id=request.attempt_id,executor_ref='measure@1',
                    status=BlockStatus.SUCCEEDED,outputs={'goal_check':{'goal_id':goal['id'],
                        'condition_hash':identity(goal['acceptance']),'subjects':subjects,
                        'execution':'VALID','evidence_refs':[str(path)],'observed':{'actual':actual,'expected':7},
                        'verdict':'VERIFIED' if actual==7 else 'FAILED'}})
        def valid_source(root,contract):
            import ast
            files=list(contract['target_scope'])
            for relative in files: ast.parse((root/relative).read_text())
            return {'passed':True,'observed':{'parsed':files},'artifacts':files}
        def snapshot(contract):
            return {p:hashlib.sha256((workspace/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() if (workspace/p).is_file() else None
                    for p in contract['target_scope']}
        def envelope(request,contract):
            measured=request.as_dict()['input_values']['execution_receipt']['outputs']['goal_check']
            return {'passed':all(acceptance.view()['subject_identities'].get(k)==v for k,v in measured['subjects'].items()),
                    'observed':measured,'evidence_refs':[dict(request.input_values['execution_receipt_ref'])]}
        client=FakeModels();events=[]
        registry=ExecutorRegistry()
        registry.register(Measure())
        registry.register(FileDeliverableChecker(workspace,{'source':valid_source}))
        registry.register(SubjectDeliverableChecker(snapshot,{'envelope':envelope}))
        catalog={'code@1':{'executor_ref':'llm.cheap@1','semantic_task_kinds':[2,3],
                           'checker_ref':'source','acceptance':['source'],'effect_domains':['source'],
                           'description':'Bounded pure function implementation and repair'},
                 'measure@1':{'executor_ref':'measure@1','semantic_task_kinds':[4],
                              'checker_ref':'measurement','acceptance':['envelope'],'effect_domains':['measurement'],
                              'description':'Independent current-object business checker'}}
        intent={'overall_goal':'return seven','user_visible_outcomes':['value() returns 7'],'constraints':['offline'],
                'forbidden_operations':['network'],'delivery_expectations':['checked local module'],
                'major_acceptance_categories':['function result']}
        bindings={'source':'deliverable.files@1','measurement':'deliverable.subjects@1'}
        if entry=='session':
            registry.register(LLMExecutor(workspace,client,ModelRoleConfig('worker','offline-worker'),
                                          semantic_protocol=True,protocol_trace=events.append))
            session=ProjectSession(AgentComposer(client,ModelRoleConfig('agent','offline-flash'),
                planner_mode='MILESTONE_MODE',protocol_trace=events.append),
                DAGRunner(registry,store,deliverable_checkers=bindings),
                store,known_capabilities=set(catalog),worker_model='offline-worker',capability_catalog=catalog,
                acceptance=acceptance,continuous=True,max_decisions=1,
                subject_snapshot=lambda paths:snapshot({'target_scope':paths}))
            result=session.run(project_id='p',run_id='r',goal='return seven',project_intent=intent)
        else:
            from pathlib import Path
            from cgkn.application.software_project.service import SoftwareProjectGenerationService
            service=SoftwareProjectGenerationService(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2],store.root,client=client)
            if entry in {'profile','conversation','api_job','local_cli'}:
                consumer=tmp_path/'consumer.py'
                consumer.write_text("import json,sys,pathlib,runpy\n"
                    "r=json.loads(pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n"
                    "p=pathlib.Path(r['workspace'])/next(iter(r['subjects']))\n"
                    "v=runpy.run_path(str(p))['value']()\n"
                    "print(json.dumps({'execution':'VALID','observed':{'value':v==7,'actual':v}}))\n")
                profile=tmp_path/'controller/.cgkn/project-session.json'
                profile.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
                profile.write_text(json.dumps({'kind':'CGKNProjectSessionProfile','schema_version':'1.0',
                    'project_id':'p','run_id':'r','workspace':str(workspace),'intent':intent,
                    'requirements':{'R-value':{'must':True,'checker_keys':['independent']}},
                    'independent_consumer':{'path':str(consumer),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(consumer.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                        'public_fields':['value','actual']},'checkpoint_interval':1}))
                if entry in {'conversation','api_job','local_cli'}:
                    from scripts import codex_conversation
                    from cgkn.application.software_project import service as service_module
                    monkeypatch.setattr(service_module,'SoftwareProjectGenerationService',lambda *a,**k:service)
                    monkeypatch.setattr(codex_conversation,'CodexConversation',lambda:pytest.fail('legacy execution entered'))
                    if entry=='conversation':

    def __init__(self, composer: AgentComposer, runner: DAGRunner, store: FileRunStore, *, known_capabilities: set[str], worker_model: str, capability_catalog: dict[str, Any] | None = None, max_decisions: int = 4, acceptance=None, continuous: bool = False, subject_snapshot=None, admission_policy: AdmissionPolicy | None = None, progress_controller: ExecutionProgressController | None = None, skill_matcher=None, skill_task=None, execution_mode='GOVERNED'):
        from veriarbor.execution_authority import ExecutionMode
        self.execution_mode=ExecutionMode(execution_mode)
        if self.execution_mode=='IMPORTED_UNMANAGED':raise ValueError('audit imports do not execute a Session')
        if self.execution_mode!=runner.execution_mode:raise ValueError('Session and DAGRunner execution modes must match explicitly')
        if self.execution_mode=='GOVERNED' and admission_policy is None:

    def test_explicit_independent_tasks_actually_overlap(tmp_path):

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/sunom/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-sunom/pytest-23496/test_process_overlap_join_and_0')

    def test_process_overlap_join_and_durable_resume(tmp_path):
        work = tmp_path / 'work'
        work.mkdir()
        registry = ExecutorRegistry()
        registry.register(CommandExecutor(work))
        def node(n,deps):
            writing=n in {'a','b'}
            c=build_leaf_contract(goal='Persist independent SQLite result '+n if writing else 'Inspect dependency databases '+n,
                kind='IMPLEMENTATION' if writing else 'TEST',scope=[n+'.finished']+([n+'/data.sqlite',n+'.started'] if writing else []),
                acceptance=['process-objects'],checker='process',output_identity=n,effect_domains=[n],executor='local.command@1',
                dependencies=deps,probe_id=n,**({'expected_delta':'new independent SQLite object and timing'} if writing else
                {'subject':'dependency database','expected_observation':'exact stored identity and digest','pass_fail_rule':'read-only SQL equality'}))
            return CapabilityNode(n, capability_ref='process@1', depends_on=deps,
            config={'executor_ref': 'local.command@1', 'parallel_safe': True,
                    'resources': {n: 'write'}, 'timeout_seconds': 15,
                    'command': [sys.executable, '-c', PROGRAM, n],'leaf_contract':c})
        nodes=tuple(node(n,deps) for n,deps in [('a',()),('b',()),('c',('a',)),('join',('b','c'))])
        tree = CapabilityTree('process integration', nodes)
        store = FileRunStore(tmp_path / 'state')
        def inspect(root,c):
            n=c['probe_id'];objects={}
            for key in ([n] if n in {'a','b'} else ['a'] if n=='c' else ['a','b']):
                with sqlite3.connect(f'file:{(root/key/"data.sqlite").as_posix()}?mode=ro',uri=True) as db:
                    objects[key]=db.execute('select * from result').fetchall()
            valid=all(rows==[(key,hashlib.sha256((key*10000).encode()).hexdigest())] for key,rows in objects.items())
            timing=json.loads((root/(n+'.finished')).read_text())
            return {'passed':valid and timing['end']>=timing['start'],'observed':{'objects':objects,'timing':timing}}
        registry.register(FileDeliverableChecker(work,{'process-objects':inspect}))
        runner = DAGRunner(registry, store, max_parallel=2,deliverable_checkers={'process':'deliverable.files@1'})
>       result = runner.run(project_id='p', run_id='r', tree=tree, dag=compile_tree(tree))
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def test_evaluation_overlaps_unrelated_business_and_route_waits(tmp_path):
        from cgkn.executors.task_evaluation import TaskEvaluationExecutor
        from cgkn.providers import ModelRoleConfig
        from tests.agent.test_capability_routing_qualification import Evaluator
        store=FileRunStore(tmp_path/'state');started=threading.Event();routed=threading.Event();times={}
        class Work:
            descriptor=ExecutorDescriptor('work@1','deterministic',('*',))
            def execute(self,r):
                times[r.node_id+'_start']=time.monotonic()
                if r.node_id=='B':started.set();assert routed.wait(10)
                if r.node_id=='route':
                    assert list((store.run_dir('p','r')/'evaluations').glob('*/*.json'));routed.set()
                times[r.node_id+'_end']=time.monotonic()
                return BlockExecutionResult(project_id='p',run_id='r',node_id=r.node_id,attempt_id=r.attempt_id,executor_ref=r.executor_ref,status=BlockStatus.SUCCEEDED,outputs={'node':r.node_id})
        class Client(Evaluator):
            def complete(self,**kw):
                assert started.wait(5);times['evaluation_start']=time.monotonic();out=super().complete(**kw);times['evaluation_end']=time.monotonic();return out
        reg=ExecutorRegistry();reg.register(Work());reg.register(TaskEvaluationExecutor(store,Client(),ModelRoleConfig('worker','fake')))

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('scope,version', [(['R'], 'qualified-revision-1'), (['S'], '1')])
    def test_scoped_revision_preserves_unrelated_checker_identity(tmp_path, scope, version, monkeypatch):
        from tests.application.software_project import test_milestone_consumer as fixtures
        acceptance_type = fixtures.GoalAcceptance
        def initial_registry(store, project, run, requirements, checkers):
            return acceptance_type(store, project, run, {**requirements, 'S': {'must': True}}, checkers)
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixtures, 'GoalAcceptance', initial_registry)
        old, request, a, workspace, original, decision, proof = fixture(tmp_path)
        proof['requirement_scope'] = scope
        decision['qualification_ref'] = put_evidence(a.store, 'p', 'r', proof)
        revision = bind_checker_revision(a.store, 'p', 'r', 'independent', original, decision)
        executor = ExternalAcceptanceExecutor(workspace, tmp_path, session_acceptance=a,
                                             consumer=original, qualified_revision=revision)
        result = executor.execute(replace(request, attempt_id='scoped'))
        assert result.succeeded
        assert result.outputs['goal_check']['checker_version'] == version
        assert result.metadata['independent_checker_hash'] == (
            revision['checker_hash'] if scope == ['R'] else original['sha256'])
        a.store.save_result(result)

        def value():
            with sqlite3.connect(f'file:{dbpath.as_posix()}?mode=ro',uri=True) as db:
